Abstract: Advanced Air Mobility (AAM) represents a transformative shift in urban transportation; however, successful implementation depends strongly on public acceptance, with noise emerging as a major concern for low-altitude electric vertical takeoff and landing (eVTOL) operations. Existing studies commonly describe eVTOL noise using acoustic metrics such as A-weighted sound level and day-night average sound level. This study develops a Geographic Information System (GIS)-based framework that translates eVTOL acoustic outputs into maps representing the percentage of the population that is highly annoyed (%HA) for a representative medical delivery route in Northwest Arkansas. The results show that noise and annoyance generally decrease with distance from the route, while the highest annoyance occurs during descent, followed by climb and cruise. Census population data are integrated to estimate the number of highly annoyed individuals and identify spatial impact hotspots. Noise-annoyance results are then combined with route distance and airspace factors to evaluate alternative routes and identify balanced routing strategies. The proposed framework connects acoustic assessment with human response and supports the identification of noise-sensitive areas, comparison of route alternatives, and socially sustainable AAM infrastructure planning.

</details>


### [215] [On the Oja-Flow-Based Low-Rank Approximation of Kalman-Bucy Filters for Linear Time-Varying Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29034)
*Kentaro Ohki*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种用于线性时变系统的低秩Kalman-Bucy滤波框架，通过Oja主成分流的跟踪分析，证明了在特定结构化假设下，Oja流可以通过调整参数保持在时变主导子空间附近，而非精确跟踪，并数值实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性时变系统中的低秩滤波缺乏理论保证，现有方法难以处理系统矩阵和噪声统计随时间变化的情况，亟需建立可分析的理论框架。

Method: 基于Oja主成分流的跟踪分析，引入对特征空间及其时间变化的结构化假设，通过调节流参数，证明Oja流能保持在时变主导子空间的邻域内，从而为低秩滤波提供理论支撑，并结合数值实验验证。

Result: 在特定假设下，Oja流能保持在时变主导子空间的邻域内；数值实验展示了该低秩滤波方法的有效性，识别了一类可处理的线性时变系统。

Conclusion: 该工作为低秩滤波在时变系统中提供了理论依据，但前提是较强的结构化假设，未来可放宽假设以扩大适用范围。

Abstract: This paper studies a low-rank Kalman-Bucy filtering framework for linear time-varying systems through the tracking analysis of Oja's principal component flow. Under structured assumptions on the eigenspaces and their time variation, we show that the Oja flow can remain in a neighborhood of the time-varying dominant subspace by tuning a parameter of the flow, rather than tracking it exactly. These restrictive assumptions identify a tractable class of linear time-varying systems and provide a theoretical basis for low-rank filtering, as illustrated by a numerical experiment.

</details>


### [216] [Machine Learning-Assisted Stability Boundary Determination of Multiport Autonomous Reconfigurable Solar Power Plants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29072)
*Qianxue Xia,Suman Debnath,Maryam Saeedifard*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于机器学习的能量平衡控制准则，用于多端口可重构太阳能发电厂（MARS）系统，以准确确定稳定性边界，确保子模块电容电压平衡，提高系统效率，并通过仿真和硬件在环测试验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MARS系统中外部电源可能导致子模块电容电压不平衡，违反多种运行条件下的稳定性约束，而现有研究未准确确定稳定性边界。

Method: 提出一种新颖的机器学习辅助能量平衡控制（EBC）判据，结合改进的EBC，控制EBC的激活与停用，确保各类子模块电容电压平衡。

Result: 通过PSCAD/EMTDC仿真和控制器硬件在环（cHIL）测试，验证了所提方法在平衡电容电压和提高系统效率方面的显著效果，并实现了高精度的稳定性边界判断。

Conclusion: 所提方法结合EBC与机器学习判据，能够实现多输入源（如MARS）系统的高效能量管理，在扩展运行范围内充分发挥系统潜力并保持高效率。

Abstract: The multiport autonomous reconfigurable solar power plant (MARS) is a promising solution to integrate renewable energy resources and energy storage systems into the ac power grid and HVdc links. In the MARS system, various input power sources are connected to the individual submodules (SMs) through dc-dc converters. However, the presence of external power sources can result in unbalanced capacitor voltages of SMs, thereby violating stability constraints under multiple/diverse operating conditions. This paper aims to address the research gap by accurately determining the stability boundary for the MARS system. A novel machine learning (ML)-assisted energy balancing control (EBC) criterion is proposed. In conjunction with a refined EBC, this approach ensures balanced capacitor voltages across various types of SMs, significantly enhancing the overall system efficiency. The proposed EBC criterion effectively controls EBC activation and deactivation, achieving remarkable accuracy. Both PSCAD/EMTDC simulations and control hardware-in-the-loop (cHIL) tests are conducted to validate the feasibility and efficiency of the proposed method. By combining the EBC and ML-assisted EBC criteria, efficient energy management becomes achievable for systems featuring multiple input power sources, such as MARS. This approach enables the system to fully exploit its potential across an expanded operational range while upholding high efficiency standards.

</details>


### [217] [Robust stabilization of time-delay discrete switched affine systems via a predictive switching control law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29143)
*Gerson Portilla,Carolina Albea,Alexandre Seuret*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对具有单个输入延迟的不确定离散时间切换仿射系统，提出了一种基于标称模型的min-switching预测控制方法，确保系统在不确定性下鲁棒收敛到极限环，并通过优化最小化吸引域大小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 切换仿射系统的控制输入为切换信号，导致闭环轨迹收敛到极限环而非平衡点。实际系统中输入延迟会破坏稳定性，现有方法未充分解决延迟与不确定性共存时的鲁棒控制问题。

Method: 基于每个模式的已知标称动力学特性，设计min-switching预测控制律；利用Lyapunov论证推导鲁棒稳定条件，并引入优化过程以最小化吸引域尺寸。

Result: 给出了可解的鲁棒稳定条件，保证系统在不确定性和延迟切换下收敛至鲁棒极限环；数值示例验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效处理了输入延迟与不确定性，实现了鲁棒极限环收敛，并可通过优化减小吸引域，为切换仿射系统提供了一种实用控制策略。

Abstract: This paper addresses the robust control of uncertain discrete-time switched affine systems subject to a single unitary input delay. The unique feature of this class of systems lies in the fact that the control input is the switching signal, which belongs to a finite set of values. Consequently, the closed-loop trajectories do not converge to an equilibrium point but rather to a limit cycle. To mitigate the impact of the input delay, we propose a min-switching predictive control approach, which is based on the known nominal dynamical characteristics of each mode. The objective of this approach is to ensure the robust stabilization of the uncertain system using this nominal predictor, employing a Lyapunov argument. Our main result provides tractable robust stabilization conditions that guarantee the convergence to a robust limit cycle under system uncertainties and delayed switching. Additionally, an optimization procedure has been incorporated to minimize the size of the attractor, which represents the region where the trajectories asymptotically converge. A numerical example validates the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [218] [Influence of Controller Tuning on Cascaded Flexibility Provision with Feedback Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29159)
*Irina Zettl,Florian Klein-Helmkamp,Florian Schmidtke,Lukas Ortmann,Andreas Ulbig*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于在线反馈优化（OFO）的级联控制在多层级电网中协调灵活性资源的影响，发现控制器参数选择对性能至关重要，并提出了指导性建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着分布式能源增多，主动配电网需为输电网提供辅助服务，而协调大量灵活性资源需要创新的控制方法。级联OFO是一种可行方案，但控制器参数如何影响整体性能尚不清楚。

Method: 通过三个案例研究，分析两级和三级OFO级联控制在配电网和输电网中的行为，评估参数化对灵活性提供精度和速度的影响，并测试模型失配和测量噪声下的参数选择。

Result: 控制器行为高度依赖级联中其他控制器的参数；参数选择显著影响灵活性提供的精度和速度；模型误差和噪声也会影响参数选取。

Conclusion: 仔细调整参数可有效实现基于OFO的级联结构对灵活性资源的垂直协调，确保高效运行。

Abstract: The coordination of a large number of flexibility-providing units across various grid layers requires innovative control concepts. This is needed, e.g., to allow active distribution systems to provide ancillary services for the transmission system. A cascaded control structure based on Online Feedback Optimization (OFO) can be used to meet flexibility requests at the point of common coupling by tracking an active power set point at the point of common coupling. This paper investigates the practical influence of the parameterization of the individual controllers on the performance of the hierarchical flexibility provision in three case studies. One case study includes a two-level controller cascade acting on one medium and two low voltage grids, and the other one includes a three-level cascade acting on low to high voltage levels. The results show that the behavior of one controller is highly dependent on the choice of control parameters of the other controllers in the cascade. Additionally, the choice of parametrization has a significant impact on the accuracy and speed of flexibility provision. A third case study investigates the effects of model mismatch and measurement noise on the appropriate selection of parameters. Overall, careful tuning enables the efficient vertical coordination of flexibility-providing units with a cascaded structure based on OFO.

</details>


### [219] [Interturn Short Circuit Fault Mitigation in PMSMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29195)
*Lukas Zezula,Matus Kozovsky,Ludek Buchta,Petr Blaha*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于控制的方法，无需额外硬件即可在匝间短路故障后继续运行标准三相永磁同步电机驱动系统。该方法通过改进磁场定向控制，生成电阻损耗最小的电流参考并重构反馈中的转矩产生电流分量。实验表明，输入功率增加减少23-36%，局部段损耗增加减少18-27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 匝间短路是永磁同步电机驱动中最严重的故障之一，导致局部过热和电流反馈失真，影响转矩控制。现有方法通常需要额外硬件或停机，本文旨在实现无额外硬件的故障后运行。

Method: 利用标准控制环信号推断的故障特征，在磁场定向控制中增加两个机制：基于离散时间故障后模型的电阻损耗最小化电流参考生成，以及在反馈路径中重构无故障转矩产生电流分量。

Result: 实验验证显示，故障引起的输入功率增加减少23-36%，局部段损耗增加减少18-27%，并能实时适应短路电阻逐步变化导致的故障恶化。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效支持故障后运行，减少损耗和发热，无需额外硬件，且能自适应故障变化。

Abstract: Interturn short circuits are among the most critical faults in permanent magnet synchronous motor drives, as they combine localized heating in the shorted stator phase with electrical asymmetry that distorts the current feedback used for torque-producing control. This article proposes a control-based mitigation method enabling the post-fault operation of standard three-phase motor drives without additional dedicated hardware. Using the diagnostic features inferred from standard control-loop signals, the method augments the field-oriented control structure with two mechanisms: resistive-loss-limited current-reference generation and reconstruction of the torque-producing current components in the feedback path. The reference generator is derived from a discrete-time post-fault model and minimizes resistive losses, whereas the feedback reconstruction provides fault-free torque-producing current components as controlled variables of the current loop. The experimental validation has demonstrated reductions of up to 23-36% in the fault-induced increase in the input power and 18-27% in the local segment-loss increase, while confirming real-time adaptation to progressive fault aggravation emulated by stepped changes in the short circuit resistance.

</details>


### [220] [Fleming-Viot Selection of the Yaglom Limit for Age-Structured Bellman-Harris Processes, with Application to Livestock Epidemic Surveillance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29251)
*Ouerdia Arezki,Paul-Marie Grollemund,Ali Zemouche*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文构造了一类次临界Bellman-Harris过程的Fleming-Viot粒子系统，证明其以多项式速率选择Yaglom极限，并建立了指数收敛的加强Yaglom定理，同时应用于牲畜流行病监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于寿命非指数，种群规模非马尔可夫，且Galton-Watson过程的Lyapunov函数不再适用，需要新的理论工具来研究次临界分支过程的Yaglom极限及其收敛速率。

Method: 在年龄配置空间上进行分析，构造Fleming-Viot粒子系统，利用非规范Lyapunov函数和漂移条件，证明Yaglom极限的选择和指数收敛速率。

Result: 证明了粒子系统以多项式速率选择Yaglom极限；条件分布以指数速率（由Malthusian参数决定）全变差收敛；漂移条件在自然Lyapunov函数类中是必要的；并应用于牲畜流行病监测的变化检测检验。

Conclusion: 该研究推广了经典Yaglom定理，揭示了漂移条件的本质，并展示了其在流行病监测中的实际应用价值。

Abstract: In this paper, we construct a Fleming-Viot particle system for a class of subcritical Bellman-Harris processes. We prove that it selects the Yaglom limit at a polynomial rate in the number of particles. Since lifetimes are non-exponential, the population size is not Markov, and the analysis must therefore be carried out on the space of age configurations. In this setting, the Lyapunov functions used for Galton-Watson processes are no longer norm-like. Nevertheless, we establish a Yaglom theorem that strengthens the classical result: the conditional laws converge in total variation at an exponential rate, with decay rate given by the Malthusian parameter. We also prove that the drift condition, which links the hazard rate to the offspring law, is necessary within a natural class of Lyapunov functions, showing that it is a feature of the measure-valued lift rather than a defect of the estimates. Finally, we illustrate the estimator through an application to livestock epidemic surveillance, where the Yaglom limit is the null distribution of a change-detection test.

</details>


### [221] [Optimal Electric Bus Depot Charging: Cost Savings, Grid Limits, and Robustness Trade-Offs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29304)
*Fabio Widmer,Luca Pinter,Mohammad Hossein Moradi,Christopher Harald Onder*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对电动公交停车场充电的凸鲁棒优化方法，在考虑行程能耗不确定性的情况下最小化电费并满足电网限制，通过瑞士四个停车场的实际调度数据验证，发现优化可显著降低电费（小停车场可降低超50%），减少电网容量需求（超40%），且鲁棒保护成本极低（小于0.1%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究虽已充分探讨成本最优充电，但缺乏对不同电价结构、电网连接容量下优化价值的量化，以及鲁棒性的经济成本评估。

Method: 采用凸鲁棒优化模型，通过最坏情况下的能量状态约束来处理有界的需求不确定性，并与充电到达策略进行对比。

Result: 在四个瑞士停车场（含7-35辆公交车）的仿真中，小停车场优化收益最大，电费降低超50%；优化可减少电网容量超40%；对10%的能耗偏差进行保护，总电费增加小于0.1%。

Conclusion: 优化充电策略能显著降低成本和电网容量需求，鲁棒保护的经济成本极低，且产生的充电功率曲线具有可解释性，可为实际应用提供指导。

Abstract: Depot charging of electric bus fleets must minimize electricity costs, respect grid limits, and remain feasible despite uncertain trip energy demand. While cost-optimal charging is well studied, its value under different electricity prices and grid connection capacities, as well as the economic cost of robustness, remain poorly quantified. We address these gaps with a convex robust formulation in which bounded demand uncertainty is enforced through worst-case state-of-energy constraints. The formulation is evaluated against charge-on-arrival using realistic service schedules for four Swiss depots containing 7-35 buses. In the depots studied, smaller depots achieve the greatest relative benefit from optimization, with total electricity cost reductions exceeding 50%, because optimization mitigates charging peaks that strongly affect their costs. For all depot sizes, the savings from optimization increase with electricity price volatility. Optimization can also lower the grid capacity required for feasible operation by over 40%, although tight limits reduce peak shaving potential. Protection against energy-demand deviations of 10% increases total electricity cost by less than 0.1%. The resulting charging power profiles exhibit interpretable price-threshold and peak-shaping behavior, providing practical guidance for real-world implementations.

</details>


### [222] [Directional Conformal Uncertainty Quantification from Learned Model Discrepancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29344)
*Cesare Donati,Fabrizio Dabbene,Martina Mammarella*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于控制中基于物理的预测器的共形预测框架，该框架通过数据驱动的差异估计定义非对称非一致性分数，生成物理一致且更窄的预测区间，并保持有限样本边际有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在控制应用中，简单模型常被优先用于合成、认证和实时使用，但这些模型并非以预测精度为唯一目标，导致其误差包含过程噪声和状态依赖的差异。需要一种方法量化此类预测器的误差，同时保持物理一致性和窄区间。

Method: 基于共形预测，利用数据驱动的差异估计（如核方法、神经网络等）定义非对称非一致性分数，使与学习差异一致的误差被低估，反之高估。构造保持原点球体，并证明对RKHS模型可使用幂函数作为局部置信度，扩展至多元Minkowski-gauge分数实现联合校准。

Result: 该框架与差异模型无关，在可交换性下保持有限样本边际有效性，并在可表征的状态-输入区域上严格缩小区间。对RKHS模型，幂函数提供自适应分数设计的局部置信度；多元扩展产生联合校准的扰动集。

Conclusion: 所提出的共形预测框架有效量化了物理预测器的误差，兼顾物理一致性、窄区间和统计保证，适用于控制应用，且能扩展至多元场景。

Abstract: We propose a conformal prediction framework for quantifying the error of physics-based predictors used in control, where simple models are preferred for synthesis, certification, and real-time use. Because these models are selected for compatibility with the intended application rather than for maximal predictive accuracy, their error combines process noise with a state-dependent discrepancy. A data-driven discrepancy estimate defines an asymmetric nonconformity score: errors consistent with the learned discrepancy are penalized less than equally large in the opposite direction. The sets remain in the nominal model's error coordinates and are physics-consistent, i.e., they contain a ball at the origin. The construction is agnostic to the discrepancy model (kernel, neural-network, or other), preserves finite-sample marginal validity under exchangeability, and provably narrows the interval over a characterizable state-input region. We further show that, for RKHS models, the power function provides a local confidence measure for adaptive score design and we extend the construction to the multivariate case via a Minkowski-gauge score yielding a jointly calibrated disturbance set.

</details>


### [223] [Memristor-Based Pulse Width Modulation Circuit for Power Converters with Programmable Frequencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29399)
*Fanfu Wu,Xiaoyi Lei,Yunting Liu*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可编程的基于忆阻器的PWM电路，工作频率约200 kHz，用于开关电源转换器，相比常用DSP实现功耗降低92%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 忆阻器在神经形态和内存计算中可大幅降低能耗，但在电源转换器控制中的集成尚不完整。现有方案要么将忆阻器输出转换为数字信号用于DSP-based PWM，重新引入计算开销；要么使用模拟PWM电路但可编程性有限。因此需要一种可编程的忆阻器PWM电路，以完全实现忆阻计算在能效方面的优势。

Method: 设计了一种结合数字控制器可编程性与模拟PWM发生器低功耗特性的忆阻器PWM电路，工作频率约200 kHz，并使用商用忆阻器进行实验验证。

Result: 实验实现的可编程开关频率范围为144.7 kHz至204.2 kHz，与常用DSP实现相比，功耗降低92%。

Conclusion: 该设计成功展示了忆阻器在电源转换器控制中的潜力，实现了高能效且可编程的PWM生成，为忆阻计算在电力电子领域的应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Memristors can achieve up to a 1000-times reduction in energy consumption in neuromorphic and in-memory computing, but their integration into power converter control remains incomplete. Existing implementations either convert memristor outputs into digital signals for DSP-based pulse-width modulation (PWM), reintroducing computational overhead, or use analog PWM circuits with limited programmability. Since PWM generation is the final essential stage of power converter control, a programmable memristor-based PWM circuit is needed to fully realize the energy-efficiency benefits of memristive computing. This paper presents a programmable memristor-based PWM circuit operating at approximately two hundred kilohertz for switching power converters. The design combines digital-controller programmability with the low power consumption of analog PWM generators. Experimental validation using commercially available memristors achieves a programmable switching frequency from 144.7 kHz to 204.2 kHz. Compared with a commonly used DSP implementation in power converter control, the proposed design achieves a 92% reduction in power consumption.

</details>


### [224] [Hybrid Virtual Space Vector Modulation With Extended Power Factor Range for Coupled Eight-Switch Three-Phase Three-Level Inverter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29416)
*Xiaojun Deng,Hongliang Wang,Tianfu Sun,Yang Jiang,Yibo Si,Wenbo Tang,Mingrui Yang,Xiumei Yue*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种适用于耦合八开关三相三电平逆变器的混合虚拟空间矢量调制（HVSVM），将功率因数范围从约[-30°,30°]扩展至[-90°,90°]，并引入主动中点电压控制（NPVC），通过实验验证了其正确性与可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耦合八开关三相三电平逆变器在光伏和电机驱动中应用广泛，但现有调制策略仅适用于高功率因数，无法满足宽功率因数范围的需求。

Method: 深入分析功率因数限制机理，在每个扇区构造两个虚拟矢量，提出三种不同功率因数范围的基本VSVM，并根据三相电流极性组合成HVSVM；同时，利用两个系数调整冗余小矢量作用时间，实现主动中点电压控制。

Result: HVSVM将功率因数角范围从约[-30°,30°]扩展到[-90°,90°]；主动NPVC能精确控制中点电压；通过3 kVA样机实验验证了所提方法的正确性和可行性。

Conclusion: 所提HVSVM和主动NPVC有效扩展了耦合八开关TP-TLI的功率因数范围并改善了中点电压控制，适用于光伏和电机驱动等宽功率因数场景。

Abstract: With the development of photovoltaic (PV) and motor drive, the coupled eight-switch three-phase three-level inverter (TP-TLI) is receiving more attentions. However, the previous modulation strategies for coupled eight-switch TP-TLI are suitable for high power factor (PF), which cannot satisfy the requirement for PV and motor drive. Aiming at this, this paper proposes a hybrid virtual space vector modulation (HVSVM) for the coupled eight switch TP-TLI with extended PF range. The mechanism of PF limitation is analyzed in deep. Two virtual vectors are constructed in each sector, and three basic VSVMs with different PF range are proposed according to the number of virtual small vectors used. Combined with three VSVMs, the HVSVM is proposed by detecting the three-phase current polarities. The PF angle range of the HVSVM is extended from about [-30°, 30°] to [-90°, 90°]. Besides, an active neutral-point voltage control (NPVC) is also presented by using two coefficients to adjust the dwell times of the two pairs of redundant small vectors. This approach considers the NP current and the derivation of NPV, and thereby it is accurate to control the NPV. The correctness and feasibility of the proposed HVSVM and active NPVC are verified by experiments through a 3 kVA prototype.

</details>


### [225] [Optimal shaping filter design for data-driven feedforward controller tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29480)
*Yusuke Fujimoto*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数据驱动的模型匹配方法，用于二自由度控制系统中的前馈控制器设计，并给出了最优整形滤波器以补偿直接辨识控制器与原始问题之间的差距，通过数值和实验验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接使用数据辨识最优控制器无法精确解决模型匹配问题，因为存在无法避免的误差或偏差，需要额外的补偿机制。

Method: 提出一种从输入输出数据构建最优整形滤波器的方法，在合理假设下利用可用信息，并讨论其与ERIT的关系。

Result: 通过数值示例和实际实验验证了所提整形滤波器的有效性，并证明了ERIT在特定情况下是最优的。

Conclusion: 所提整形滤波器能够有效解决数据驱动模型匹配问题，且ERIT可作为其特例，为实际控制设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: This paper discusses the data-driven model matching problem. In particular, this paper focuses on two-degree-of-freedom control systems, and consider to design feedforward controller from input-output data. An intuitive solution to this problem would be identifying the optimal controller using data, but this does not give the exact solution to the original problem. A shaping filter is required to compensate for this gap, and the main contribution of this paper is to give the optimal shaping filter. The proposed shaping filter is constructed from available information under reasonable assumptions, and its effectiveness is shown through a numerical example and a practical experiment. The relation between the proposed shaping filter and Estimated Response Iterative Tuning (ERIT) is also discussed, and it is shown that ERIT is optimal for a special case.

</details>


### [226] [Quantum Inspired QUBO Assisted ALNS for Reliability Driven Hurricane Restoration of Distribution Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29544)
*Hooman Torkaman,Jignesh Solanki,Anurag Srivastava*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出一种量子启发的QUBO辅助自适应大邻域搜索框架，用于飓风后配电网修复调度，在IEEE 123节点测试馈线上相比经典ALNS显著缩短恢复时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 飓风后配电网恢复需要快速修复调度，但受馈线拓扑、现场物流和电气可行性约束，经典方法在严重损坏时组合空间大，调度效率低。

Method: 结合QUBO模型与ALNS框架，使用本地CPU模拟退火采样对修复任务排序，确定性解码器保证卡车物流和资源利用，并通过OpenDSS回放验证调度方案。

Result: 在100m/s风灾情景下，相比经典ALNS，平均系统平均中断持续时间指数和未供电能量降低2.24%，恢复完成时间缩短50.71%。

Conclusion: QUBO辅助在严重损坏导致更大组合修复空间时价值最大，能有效提升恢复调度效率。

Abstract: Post hurricane distribution system restoration requires rapid repair scheduling subject to feeder topology, field logistics, and electrical feasibility. This paper presents a quantum inspired quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) assisted adaptive large neighborhood search (ALNS) framework. At each restoration stage, a local CPU simulated annealing sampler ranks individual repairs and multi job combinations near the energized frontier. A deterministic decoder preserves crew truck logistics, enforces full useful crew utilization, and rejects infeasible batches. Final schedules are validated through OpenDSS replay. The framework is evaluated on the IEEE 123 node test feeder without distributed generation under 80, 90, and 100 m/s wind scenarios. In the 100 m/s stress test, the proposed method reduces mean system average interruption duration index and energy not supplied by 2.24% and restoration makespan by 50.71% relative to classical energized ALNS. Results show that QUBO assistance is most valuable when severe damage creates a larger combinatorial repair space.

</details>


### [227] [Profit-Driven Pricing and SLA-Aware Reserve Sizing for Multi-Tenant Satellite O-RAN Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29594)
*Manobendu Sarker,Gunes Karabulut Kurt,Wael Jaafar*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了卫星O-RAN批发场景下的多租户资源分配问题，提出联合定价与储备分配的交替优化方法，在满足SLA约束下实现近最优利润（差距约1%），并将运行时间减少22倍，同时提高资源利用率30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星O-RAN批发场景中，异构流量共享时变且有限的卫星容量，需同时考虑价格弹性需求和严格的SLA约束，但现有研究未能有效联合优化经济与可靠性因素。

Method: 将概率SLA约束转化为确定性边际约束，构建非凸的联合定价与储备分配问题，并采用交替优化（AO）分离定价与分配决策，保证可行性和SLA。

Result: 该方法利润接近全局最优（差距约1%），运行时间减少22倍；基线方案利润损失超15%或违反SLA。新方法保持非正SLA差距，资源利用率较无储备控制的基线提高30%。

Conclusion: 联合经济与资源控制可高效利用稀缺卫星容量，实现可靠服务与可扩展计算，为卫星O-RAN批发提供有效方案。

Abstract: This paper studies a multi-tenant resource allocation problem in a satellite open radio access network (O-RAN) wholesale setting, where heterogeneous traffic classes share a time-varying limited satellite capacity over a finite planning horizon. We formulate a joint pricing and reserve allocation problem from a service provider perspective, where tenant-specific demand exhibits price elasticity and stochastic service requirements subject to strict service-level agreement (SLA) constraints, leading to a coupled economic and reliability-driven bottleneck. A deterministic reformulation is adopted to approximate probabilistic SLA requirements through tractable margin constraints, enabling coordinated control of horizon-wide contract prices and time-varying reserves. The resulting problem is non-convex due to interdependent decisions across tenants, time windows, and service classes. To address this, an alternating optimization (AO) scheme is developed separating pricing and allocation decisions while preserving feasibility and SLA guarantees. Numerical results show that the proposed method achieves near-optimal profit within approximately $1\%$ of a global benchmark, while reducing runtime by up to $22\times$. In contrast, considered baseline schemes incur profit losses exceeding $15\%$ or fail to satisfy SLA constraints. The proposed approach consistently maintains non-positive empirical SLA gaps and achieves up to $30\%$ higher resource utilization than a price-optimization baseline without adaptive reserve control. These results demonstrate that joint economic and resource control enables the provider to efficiently exploit scarce satellite network capacity with reliable service delivery and scalable computation.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [228] [Simulative Anomaly Detection using 2D Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28701)
*Moti Ben-Harush,Nimrod Teneh,Gregory Lukovsky*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出一种预测生物组织内部异常（如癌细胞）成像质量的新技术，用于评估和设计射频断层扫描传感器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 射频断层扫描在检测体内异常时，成像质量受多种因素影响，缺乏有效的预测手段，导致传感器设计困难。

Method: 该技术通过分析传感器参数和介质特性，结合数值模拟或理论模型，定量预测异常目标的成像质量。

Result: 能够有效预测不同条件下异常目标的成像质量，为传感器评估和设计提供依据。

Conclusion: 该预测技术有助于优化射频断层扫描系统的设计，提高对体内异常的检测能力。

Abstract: We present a novel technique for predicting the imaging quality of anomalies such as cancer cells located inside organic tissues. This technique is useful for evaluating and designing RF tomography sensors.

</details>


### [229] [MoPET: Parameter-Efficient Mixture-of-Experts for Unified Medical Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29462)
*Sebastian Doerrich,Daniel Würtinger,Francesco Di Salvo,Shyam Nandan Rai,Christian Ledig*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出MoPET方法，通过混合专家和稀疏路由整合多个PEFT适配器到单一网络，避免负迁移，提升异构医学影像数据集的平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数高效微调（PEFT）虽适应有限数据，但为每个任务训练独立适配器，整合时因梯度冲突导致负迁移。

Method: MoPET基于冻结基础模型，注入低秩PEFT专家，用学习稀疏路由选择子集，实现跨数据集共享能力并减少梯度冲突。

Result: 在MedMNIST上，PEFT优于全量微调（88.97% vs 86.50%）；单一MoPET整合四数据集平均准确率93.46% vs 孤立适配器92.83%；辅助数据联合训练使目标准确率从81.58%升至83.58%。

Conclusion: MoPET有效统一异构临床诊断任务，缓解负迁移，提升数据受限场景的性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Adapting deep learning models to profound clinical heterogeneity typically relies on parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) to avoid the severe overfitting associated with full end-to-end network updates. Although PEFT successfully navigates limited data scenarios, it inherently forces the training of a separate, isolated adapter for every specific diagnostic task. Consolidating these isolated adapters into a single generalist network risks negative transfer, as optimization gradients from conflicting visual domains interfere. To address this, we propose MoPET, a mixture-of-experts (MoE) method that uses a learned sparse router to direct each input through a small subset of low-rank PEFT experts injected into a frozen foundation model, sharing capacity across datasets while limiting cross-domain gradient conflict. Through selected evaluations on the MedMNIST benchmark, we first establish that PEFT outperforms full network updates, improving average accuracy from 86.50% to 88.97%. We then show that a single MoPET model consolidates four heterogeneous datasets into one network, improving average accuracy over the best isolated PEFT adapters (93.46% versus 92.83%). Finally, we show that co-training with auxiliary datasets improves accuracy on data-constrained clinical targets, raising average target accuracy over the strongest isolated adapter from 81.58% to 83.58%. Our source code is publicly available at https://github.com/sdoerrich97/mopet .

</details>


### [230] [Few-shot Deep Learning for Phase-Amplitude Aberration Correction in Transcranial Focused Ultrasound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29182)
*Minju Seol,Minjee Seo,Seonaeng Cho,Kyungho Yoon*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于深度学习的少样本框架，从CT图像快速预测经颅聚焦超声的相位和幅度校正，速度比传统时间反转模拟快2535倍，精度高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时间反转模拟计算昂贵，无法实时应用，需要快速、准确的患者特异性校正方法。

Method: 几何感知编码器提取颅骨路径特征，分别用于相位分类和幅度回归；相位周期用循环期望解码处理；通过预训练和微调（仅需10个目标点）适应新患者。

Result: 在12个颅骨上交叉验证：相位误差0.155 rad，幅度误差9.089%，焦点偏移0.467 mm，Dice 94.422%，峰值压力比92.332%，速度提升约2535倍。

Conclusion: 该框架能快速、准确地校正tFUS波前畸变，有望用于实时治疗计划。

Abstract: Transcranial focused ultrasound (tFUS) is a non-invasive technique that delivers focused acoustic energy through the skull for neuromodulation and therapeutic applications. However, the heterogeneous structure of the skull induces complex, patient-specific phase and amplitude aberrations that distort the acoustic focus and deviate it from the intended target, compromising therapeutic efficacy and safety. Conventional time-reversal (TR) simulations can correct these aberrations but rely on computationally expensive full-wave solvers, making them impractical for real-time use and iterative treatment planning. We propose a few-shot deep surrogate framework that predicts per-element phase and amplitude corrections for a 96-element 3D phased-array transducer from patient CT images. A geometry-aware encoder extracts skull-path features shared across dedicated phase classification and amplitude regression branches, where phase periodicity is handled via circular expectation decoding. The framework is pretrained on diverse skull geometries and fine-tuned with only ten target points, enabling rapid adaptation to unseen patients without full patient-specific simulation. Evaluated via leave-one-out cross-validation across 12 skulls, it achieves a mean phase CMAE of 0.155 rad and amplitude rMAE of 9.089%, a focal centroid error of 0.467 mm, Dice score of 94.422%, and peak pressure ratio of 92.332%, with an approximately 2,535 times speedup over TR simulation. The code is available at https://github.com/Minju-Seol/fewshot-tfus-correction.

</details>
